	tree = we_high_2_tree

		2
		2
	}

	parent = we_high_2_1_perk
	can_be_picked = {
		we_can_learn_high = yes
		has_perk = we_high_0_2_perk
		has_perk = we_high_1_2_perk

		we_can_learn_high = yes

		we_magic_graduate_effect = yes
	}
	character_modifier = {
		learning = 2
	}

		value = 10
